- **sequence-diagram**: Use during high-level-design or writing-plans for any operation that crosses 3+ components or service boundaries. Reads the HLD Container diagram and spec REQ-NNN to identify critical flows, generates Mermaid sequenceDiagram blocks for each (happy path, error paths, auth boundary, async vs sync), and saves to .ai/YYYY-MM-DD-&lt;feature-slug&gt;/lld/lld-&lt;feature-slug&gt;-sequences.md. Runs sequence-diagram-reviewer before committing. Without sequence diagrams, multi-service bugs are found during debugging, not design. ([CagesThrottleUs/private-ai-harness](https://github.com/CagesThrottleUs/private-ai-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/sequence-diagram))
- **sequence-diagram**: Generate PlantUML sequence diagrams saved as .adoc files by tracing how systems communicate through an API endpoint's code. Use this skill whenever the user wants to draw, generate, create, visualize, or document a sequence diagram for an API endpoint, flow, or feature. - **sequence-diagram**: ソースコード・設計書・処理仕様・自然言語の説明から、メソッド単位のライフラインで処理を正確に追跡した Mermaid シーケンス図を作り、クリックで引数・戻り値・SQL・根拠のソース行を確認できる横長の HTML ビューア（単一ファイル）として出力する。条件分岐・繰り返し・例外・トランザクション・排他ロック・非同期と並列・DB アクセス・全体図と詳細図の分割に対応し、推定と確認済みを区別する。「シーケンス図を作って」「処理の流れを図にして」「この API／メソッドの呼び出し関係を可視化して」「Mermaid で処理フロー」「sequence diagram」など、処理の時系列や呼び出し関係を図で説明したい依頼では、言語・フレームワークを問わずこのスキルを使う。フローチャート・ER 図・クラス図だけが欲しい場合は対象外。 ([yu-ki-m/super-intelligence](https://github.com/yu-ki-m/super-intelligence/tree/HEAD/SKILL/.claude/skills/sequence-diagram))

Invoquer à la fin de toute création ou modification sous courses/, exercices/ ou scenarios/, et quand on demande de vérifier ou critiquer une séance. ([Maus3rSR/K.School](https://github.com/Maus3rSR/K.School/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sequencing-audit))
- **sequencing-background-noise-evaluation**: Use when hotspot mutation calls from converted nanopore short reads must be qualified by an empirically measured background noise profile and LoD before being trusted for clinical hotspot panels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-background-noise-evaluation))
- **sequencing-base-masks**: Sequencing read requirements and bcl2fastq base masks for 10x Genomics libraries: chemistry-specific BasesMaskCR values, ATAC I2 barcode requirement (capital I not lowercase y), Visium FFPE vs frozen R2 length difference. ([CodingKaiser/kaiser-skills](https://github.com/CodingKaiser/kaiser-skills/tree/HEAD/sequencing-base-masks) · ★6)
- **sequencing-bets**: Orders more ideas than capacity into Now, Next and Later against ranked problems, by opportunity cost, evidence and what unblocks learning, with a stop rule per bet and a Not doing list. Use when asked what to build first or next, planning a quarter or roadmap, or facing competing requests. Not for shaping one idea (shaping-direction). ([bymw/mstack](https://github.com/bymw/mstack/tree/HEAD/skills/sequencing-bets))
- **sequencing-changes**: Order a change across commits, MRs and deploys so nothing breaks in between: expand and contract for contracts crossing a deploy boundary, a replacement built beside the old feature, feature flags, prefactoring. Use when splitting work into commits or MRs, writing a migration, removing or renaming a column, API field, event payload or stored key, or replacing a feature or page. ([nash403/skills](https://github.com/nash403/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sequencing-changes))
- **sequencing-cost-estimation**: Use when a semi-profiling workflow needs a defensible dollar figure for sequencing only representative samples versus the whole cohort, or when judging whether the 1/10–1/3 cost-savings target of scSemiProfiler was achieved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-cost-estimation))
- **sequencing-cost-modeling-droplet-platforms**: Use when planning a multi-sample single cell DE or eQTL study under a fixed budget and you must decide the trade-off between cells per individual, sequencing depth (reads/cell), and number of samples on a droplet (10X) platform. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-cost-modeling-droplet-platforms))
- **sequencing-coverage-downsampling**: Use when benchmarking how coverage depth affects strain separation quality, e.g. building a Mock3 5x–50x downsampling series of simulated PacBio reads before strain-oblivious assembly and Strainberry separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-coverage-downsampling))
- **sequencing-coverage-parameter-sweep**: Use when planning or evaluating 10x Chromium Linked-Read library preparation/sequencing parameters (CF, CR, C, fragment length) and you need to know which combinations maximize contig/scaffold N50 and NA50 for diploid de novo assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-coverage-parameter-sweep))
- **sequencing-data-handling**: Handle biological sequence data in FASTA, FASTQ, VCF, GFF3, BED, and SAM formats. Use when the user provides sequence files, asks about sequence formats, needs format conversion, or performs operations like complement, reverse-complement, or translation. Also trigger when other skills need sequence parsing support. ([mark64oswald/lexa-bioscience-public](https://github.com/mark64oswald/lexa-bioscience-public/tree/HEAD/skills/patterns/sequence))
- **sequencing-depth-accuracy-convergence-analysis**: Decision support for choosing between 16S+PICRUSt prediction and shallow shotgun sequencing: use when paired 16S OTU tables and HUMAnN-annotated KO tables from the same samples are available and the question is accuracy as a function of sequencing depth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-accuracy-convergence-analysis))
- **sequencing-depth-correlated-dimension-exclusion**: Use when processing sci-L3-RNA/ATAC count matrices with Seurat/Signac and cell assignments must be validated against SNV ground truth — deep-sequenced cells otherwise separate on a depth-driven component. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-correlated-dimension-exclusion))
- **sequencing-depth-downsampling**: Use when validating that a Hi-C compartment/sub-compartment inference method recovers stable scores at shallow sequencing depths, by simulating trans-interaction summary files at multiple downsampled depths (e.g. 1x, 0.1x, 0.01x). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-downsampling--2ef7682135974888))
- **sequencing-depth-downsampling**: Use when validating that a broad-domain caller (e.g., RECOGNICER on H3K27me3/H3K9me3 ChIP-seq) is robust to sequencing depth by downsampling mapped reads and comparing aggregate domain length and FRIP across depths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-downsampling--676a88f58b7c11ec))
- **sequencing-depth-downsampling**: Use when benchmarking a long-read ARG profiling or taxonomic classification pipeline's sensitivity to sequencing depth, requiring matched subsets of reads spanning low (1–2×) to high (32×) coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-downsampling))
- **sequencing-depth-downsampling-simulation**: Decision support for whether a ChIP-seq/DNase-seq QC metric like FRiP is a genuine quality readout or merely an artifact of sequencing depth, especially when benchmarking shallow libraries or choosing downsampling conventions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-downsampling-simulation))
- **sequencing-depth-fold-change-simulation**: Use when validating that a single-cell cluster-purity metric (e.g., ROGUE) is insensitive to sequencing-depth fold changes between replicates or to variation in cell number, by simulating replicate mixtures and comparing ROGUE/S against silhouette. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-fold-change-simulation))
- **sequencing-depth-matched-model-selection**: Use when scoring/predicting chromatin loops on a new Hi-C, Micro-C, or DNA SPRITE contact map (.cool/.hic) in a cell type or species different from the training set, and a depth-matched pre-trained model must be chosen. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-matched-model-selection))
- **sequencing-depth-normalization**: Use when computing normalized ChIP-seq signal (buildChIPprofile, recoverChIPlevels, meta-plots, boxplots) from BAM files of samples sequenced at different depths, or when down-sampling to test depth requirements. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-normalization))
- **sequencing-depth-normalization-comparison**: Use when pairwise metagenome distances must resolve communities differing in sequencing depth, dominant-organism abundance, or genetic composition, and MinHash sketches (Mash) or presence/absence metrics (Simka Jaccard) compress or saturate those differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-normalization-comparison))
- **sequencing-depth-perturbation-analysis**: Use when a single-cell velocity model's outputs must be shown stable under technical noise, specifically when perturbing raw count matrices by downsampling to reduced sequencing-depth proportions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-perturbation-analysis))
- **sequencing-depth-rarefaction-control**: Use when validating a longitudinal differential-abundance method against sequencing-depth bias: you need negative-control data with no true time effect but varying library sizes, or must quantify FPR inflation from compositional normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-rarefaction-control))
- **sequencing-depth-regression-normalization**: Use when a SnapATAC cell-by-cell Jaccard kernel must be normalized for per-cell sequencing-depth bias before spectral dimensionality reduction of scATAC-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-regression-normalization))
- **sequencing-depth-robustness-analysis**: Use when evaluating loop-calling model robustness on downsampled Hi-C contact maps, or when comparing graph-view vs image-view branch performance across sequencing depths (e.g., 1.5% / 39M reads up to 520M reads). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-robustness-analysis))
- **sequencing-depth-saturation-analysis**: Use when you have a high-coverage dRNA-seq dataset (e.g. PromethION human reads, SRA BioProject PRJNA995902) and need to quantify how sequencing depth affects m6A tool performance (hits, F1, AUPRC). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-saturation-analysis))
- **sequencing-depth-saturation-analysis**: Use when planning or justifying sequencing depth for shotgun metatranscriptomes (e.g., human gut stool samples), or when asked to demonstrate via subsampling how many annotated reads are required to reach a target accuracy for low-abundance transcripts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-saturation-analysis))
- **sequencing-depth-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when you need to quantify how a viral metagenomic classifier/reconstruction pipeline (e.g. VirMAP on VMC reads) degrades as sequencing depth is reduced, or to compare retention of a reconstruction pipeline against a standard read-classification baseline at shallow depth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-sensitivity-analysis))
- **sequencing-depth-stratified-recall-evaluation**: Use when a large single-cell dataset with deposited predefined cell labels is available and you need to quantify whether per-cell UMI counts (sequencing depth) bias the accuracy of an automatic annotation tool such as deCS.correlation(). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-stratified-recall-evaluation))
- **sequencing-depth-subsampling-assessment**: Use when evaluating whether richness estimates and rarefaction curves from finished DADA2 ASV tables can be trusted, especially for mock community data subsampled across sequencing depths (e.g. 100–1,600,000 read pairs or 100–40,000 reads). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/sequencing-depth-subsampling-assessment))
- **sequencing-depth-variance-simulation**: Use when evaluating how an estimator (e.g., allelic proportion or expression estimates) performs at reduced sequencing depth, by simulating low-coverage data via random per-cell read down-sampling from full-depth counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-depth-variance-simulation))
- **sequencing-engine**: Проектирование и контроль систем управления ходом времени, циклов симуляции, обновления сущностей и бесконфликтного обмена кадрами/состояниями в реальном времени. ([LeonidLR/Godot_Test_01](https://github.com/LeonidLR/Godot_Test_01/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sequencing-engine))
- **sequencing-error-correction**: Use when exact-overlap-based read recruitment (e.g., TAR-VIR overlap extension with an overlap cutoff) is planned downstream, since raw sequencing errors would break exact overlap matching between reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-correction))
- **sequencing-error-injection-simulation**: Use when benchmarking tandem-repeat decomposition accuracy against a known ground truth, e.g. simulating (AC)m(AG)n-style mosaic TRs with substitution error injection before scoring predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-injection-simulation))
- **sequencing-error-markov-chain-modeling**: Use when benchmarking isoform quantification methods (e.g. Longcell, Sicelore2, FLAMES, wf-single-cell, IsoQuant) on simulated Nanopore reads where per-read error processes must be modeled at controlled low/middle/high quality regimes with a ground-truth cell-by-isoform matrix for scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-error-markov-chain-modeling))
- **sequencing-error-metrics-tabulation**: Use when comparing simulated nanopore read quality across simulators (e.g. seq2squiggle vs squigulator) on R10.4.1 datasets and you need median match rate and median PHRED score per organism/mode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-metrics-tabulation))
- **sequencing-error-model-configuration**: Use when you must generate simulated shotgun metagenomic FASTQ benchmarks at prescribed mean depths with an instrument-specific error model (NovaSeq) for validating variant-discovery tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-model-configuration))
- **sequencing-error-modeling-gene-calling**: Use when gene-calling on unassembled metagenomic reads from error-prone platforms (e.g. 454 with homopolymer-run indels), where frameshifts in raw reads break standard gene predictors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-modeling-gene-calling))
- **sequencing-error-probability-modeling**: Use when assembling overlapping Illumina paired-end reads (e.g. 16S rRNA amplicons in FASTQ/SAM) where naive perfect-match overlap discards ~40% of reads due to uncalled or miscalled bases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-probability-modeling))
- **sequencing-error-rate-benchmarking**: Use when raw PacBio CLR read sets (e.g., yeast pseudo-diploid, NWC metagenome, Microbial 10-plex) must be corrected and their residual sequencing error rate computed to reproduce or compare against published correction benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-rate-benchmarking))
- **sequencing-error-rate-estimation**: Use when you have parent sequences and their one-mismatch children from amplicon fastq data and need to estimate the Illumina substitution error rate or verify that one-mismatch child accumulation follows a Poisson process. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-rate-estimation))
- **sequencing-error-rate-estimation-by-pass-number**: Use when quantifying PacBio CCS read accuracy across circular-consensus pass numbers, or when deciding how many CCS passes are needed before full-length 16S sequence variants can be trusted. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-rate-estimation-by-pass-number))
- **sequencing-error-rate-quantification**: Use when error-prone NGS reads (e.g., 454 Titanium pyrosequencing of HIV-1 subtype B pol) from a genetically heterogeneous sample must be error-corrected before estimating haplotype frequencies or clone prevalence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-rate-quantification))
- **sequencing-error-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking or validating UMI recovery/collapse methods (e.g., Longcell iterative clustering vs DBSCAN eps=2) where you need error-laden simulated reads with known ground-truth UMI counts across amplification levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-error-read-simulation))
- **sequencing-error-region-sequence-extraction**: Use when you have genomic coordinates of systematic sequencing errors (e.g. the 3272 monocyte SSE positions on Hg18) and need paired signal/control FASTA inputs for fixed-position IUPAC motif discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-error-region-sequence-extraction))
- **sequencing-error-threshold-calibration**: Use when generating error-tolerant DNA barcode kits for nanopore sequencing and you need to translate a target sequencing error rate and tolerated error count into PRO's probability-divergence threshold t. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-error-threshold-calibration))
- **sequencing-fastq-input-handling**: Use when raw nanopore sequencing data in FASTQ format must be imported into Galaxy (e.g., from a GigaDB dataset) and characterized before downstream long-read assembly or AMR analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-fastq-input-handling))
- **sequencing-fits-for-early-stage-growth**: Sequencing the Four Fits for early-stage growth sets the order to establish each fit, starting from the market and looping back as evidence comes in. ([gethamster/skills](https://github.com/gethamster/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sequencing-fits-for-early-stage-growth) · ★4)
- **sequencing-for-launch**: Use when prioritizing a large or multi-repo backlog, ordering what to build next, building a roadmap, scoping an MVP or launch cut, deciding what to defer, or weighing a partial/shim/stopgap against building the real thing. Symptoms: "hundreds of tickets", cross-repo dependencies, "what should we do first", "is this worth shimming for now". ([MindFortressInc/reeve-public-coding-harness](https://github.com/MindFortressInc/reeve-public-coding-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/sequencing-for-launch) · ★3)
- **sequencing-fragment-extraction**: Use when preparing fragment files for haplotype assembly tools (SpecHap, HapCUT2, FastHare, DCHap, ReFHap) from BAM/CRAM alignments and bgzipped VCF variant calls across diverse sequencing platforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-fragment-extraction))
- **sequencing-instrument-swapping-control-comparison**: Use when multiplexed 10x Genomics droplet libraries have been sequenced on both a patterned-flowcell instrument (HiSeq 4000/HiSeq X/NovaSeq) and a non-patterned control (HiSeq 2500), and you need to quantify or rule out excess inter-sample cell-barcode sharing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-instrument-swapping-control-comparison))
- **sequencing-noise-modeling**: Use when building simulated single-cell sequencing benchmarks that must preserve haplotype structure and amplification artifacts while challenging BAF/phasing-based CNA callers with realistic noise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-noise-modeling))
- **sequencing-pipelines**: Use when building or validating sequencing analysis: short- and long-read QC, alignment, variant calling, benchmarking, assembly, pangenomes, and workflow managers. Not for tests and CI (pipeline-testing-and-ci). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/sequencing-pipelines))
- **sequencing-platform-error-profile-analysis**: Use when gene-predicting short metagenomic reads from error-prone platforms (454/Sanger with indels, Illumina with substitutions), especially when truncated ORFs and frame shifts from homopolymer-run artifacts must be resolved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-platform-error-profile-analysis))
- **sequencing-platform-parameter-configuration**: Use when running VeChat (or its Minimap2 overlap step) on raw long reads and the input platform (PacBio PB or ONT) must be declared so platform-appropriate alignment presets and error models are applied. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-platform-parameter-configuration))
- **sequencing-platform-swap-control-analysis**: Use when a barcode-swapping fraction has been estimated on a patterned flow-cell instrument (HiSeq 4000/X/NovaSeq) and you need a same-libraries control on a non-patterned instrument (HiSeq 2500) to attribute swapping to the sequencer rather than the libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-platform-swap-control-analysis))
- **sequencing-protocol-stratified-benchmarking**: Use when a cell type classifier trained on 10x 3'/5' scRNA-seq must be evaluated on cells from other sequencing protocols (STRT-seq, Smart-seq2, BD Rhapsody Targeted mRNA) to quantify protocol-specific generalization loss. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-protocol-stratified-benchmarking))
- **sequencing-quality-filtering**: Use when raw Illumina paired-end amplicon FASTQ reads must be merged, primer-trimmed, and expected-error-filtered before dereplication, chimera removal, or OTU/ASV clustering in an EasyAmplicon-style pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/sequencing-quality-filtering))
- **sequencing-read-alignment**: Use when simulated (wgsim/LRSIM, PBSIM/DeepSimulator) or real sequencing reads must be mapped to a reference genome and produced as sorted, indexed BAM files as a prerequisite for fragment extraction (extractHAIRS) and haplotype assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-read-alignment))
- **sequencing-read-alignment-filtering**: Use when Illumina FASTQ reads (real or ART-simulated) must be mapped to a fungal taxonomic database and off-target bacterial mapping must be quantified or minimized via strict alignment-length and quality thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-read-alignment-filtering))
- **sequencing-read-quality-assessment**: Use when starting a sequencing analysis (DNA-seq or RNA-seq) from raw FASTQ data and the downstream pipeline needs quality-checked, adapter-free, length- and quality-filtered reads before alignment or genotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-read-quality-assessment))
- **sequencing-read-quality-control**: Use when raw metagenomic samples arrive as SRA archives and must be converted to FASTQ/FASTA, quality-checked, and summarized with sequence statistics before mapping in the CAMAMED pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sequencing-read-quality-control))
- **sequencing-read-region-property-checking**: Use when a seqspec specification (YAML) file for a genomics assay library must be schema-validated before use in preprocessing, or when auditing a curated library of assay specs for missing required Region fields. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sequencing-read-region-property-checking))
